PST QuickView development updater/runtime notices

This supplement retains published license/notice files for the new or changed
lockfile dependency closure reached from the updater, HTTP/TLS, HMAC, version
parser and macOS Keychain bridge, plus the updated JavaScript runtime API.
The scope is deliberately conservative: optional, build-only and non-macOS
packages may be listed. This does not claim all listed packages are linked into
every app slice or complete pre-existing application license compliance.

Archive digests were checked against Cargo.lock or package-lock.json before
reading these files. Identical license texts are stored once by SHA-256 and
referenced by each package inventory entry. Complete embedded third-party
notices are preserved. A package with no separate license file in its published
archive retains its declared identifier and available source attribution
headers; no missing copyright notice has been invented. This supplement is
not a replacement for ReadPST Corresponding Source or legal certification.

Exact published Rust source archives can be obtained at
https://static.crates.io/crates/NAME/NAME-VERSION.crate using the component
name/version and SHA-256 recorded below. The npm runtime API source archive is
https://registry.npmjs.org/@tauri-apps/api/-/api-VERSION.tgz, using its recorded
version and SHA-512. These public package source locations do not replace any
separate source-distribution obligations for the complete application.
